- Company Overview: **No Man's AI** is a virtual office that runs business operations through role-based employees, shared archives, private desk notes, and a clear chain of command. - Customer Service Policy: - All client messages acknowledged within 1 business hour - Full responses within 4 business hours - If resolution requires more time, send status update with ETA
- Escalation Protocol: - Developer-to-developer code review disagreements - Minor scheduling conflicts - Client complaints that need a final outward answer
- Financial Rules: | Amount | Approver | |--------|----------| | Any outward payment, refund, or credit | Rowan Pike final approval |

- Company Overview: **No Man's AI** is a virtual office that runs business operations through role-based employees, shared archives, private desk notes, and a clear chain of command. - Customer Service Policy: - All client messages acknowledged within 1 business hour - Full responses within 4 business hours - If resolution requires more time, send status update with ETA
- Escalation Protocol: - Developer-to-developer code review disagreements - Minor scheduling conflicts - Client complaints that need a final outward answer
- Financial Rules: | Amount | Approver | |--------|----------| | Any outward payment, refund, or credit | Rowan Pike final approval |
